// STORE NAVIGATION
// ══════════════════════════════════════════
function showSection(id) {
    document.getElementById('adminPanel').classList.remove('active');
    document.getElementById('publicNav').style.display = '';
    document.getElementById('publicFooter').style.display = '';
    document.querySelectorAll('.section').forEach(s => s.classList.remove('active'));
    document.getElementById(id).classList.add('active');
    renderCurrentUser();
    if (id === 'perfil') syncUserInputsFromCurrentUser();
    if (id === 'carrito') renderCart();
    if (id === 'misPedidos') renderOrders();
    if (id === 'catalogo') renderProducts();
    if (id === 'home') { renderHeroVisual(); renderOfertas(); updateHeroStats(); }
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
}

function toggleDropdown() { document.getElementById('dropdownMenu').classList.toggle('show'); }
document.addEventListener('click', e => {
    if (!e.target.closest('#userBtn') && !e.target.closest('#dropdownMenu'))
        document.getElementById('dropdownMenu').classList.remove('show');
});

// ══════════════════════════════════════════
// TOAST
// ══════════════════════════════════════════
function toast(msg, type = '') {
    const el = document.createElement('div');
    el.className = 'toast ' + type;
    el.textContent = msg;
    document.getElementById('toastContainer').appendChild(el);
    setTimeout(() => el.remove(), 3200);
}

function starsMarkup(count) {
    return Array.from({length:5}, (_,i) => `<i class="bi ${i < count ? 'bi-star-fill' : 'bi-star'}"></i>`).join('');
}

// ══════════════════════════════════════════
// HERO / HOME
// ══════════════════════════════════════════
function renderHeroVisual() {
    const active = PRODUCTS.filter(p => p.active);
    const cards = active.slice(0, 4);
    document.getElementById('heroVisual').innerHTML = cards.map(p => `
        <div class="hero-card" onclick="openModal(${p.id})">
            <div class="hero-card-badge"><i class="bi bi-star-fill"></i> Top</div>
            ${p.img ? `<img src="${p.img}" style="width:60px;height:60px;object-fit:cover;border-radius:8px;margin-bottom:0.5rem">` : `<div class="hero-card-emoji">${p.emoji}</div>`}
            <div class="hero-card-name">${p.name}</div>
            <div class="hero-card-price">$${p.price.toLocaleString('es-CO')}</div>
        </div>`).join('');
}

function updateHeroStats() {
    document.getElementById('heroStatProducts').textContent = PRODUCTS.filter(p=>p.active).length + '+';
}

function renderOfertas() {
    document.getElementById('ofertasGrid').innerHTML = OFFERS.map(o => {
        const p = PRODUCTS.find(pr => pr.id === o.id);
        if (!p || !p.active) return '';
        const discounted = Math.round(p.price * (1 - o.discount / 100));
        return `<div class="oferta-card" onclick="openModal(${p.id})">
            ${p.img ? `<img class="oferta-card-img" src="${p.img}">` : `<div class="oferta-card-emoji">${p.emoji}</div>`}
            <div class="oferta-card-name">${p.name}</div>
            <div class="oferta-prices">
                <span class="price-old">$${p.price.toLocaleString('es-CO')}</span>
                <span class="price-new">$${discounted.toLocaleString('es-CO')}</span>
                <span class="discount-badge">-${o.discount}%</span>
            </div>
            <button class="oferta-btn" onclick="event.stopPropagation();addToCart(${p.id})">Agregar al carrito</button>
        </div>`;
    }).join('');
}

// ══════════════════════════════════════════
// PRODUCTS (store)
// ══════════════════════════════════════════
function renderProducts(list) {
    const products = (list || PRODUCTS).filter(p => p.active);
    const grid = document.getElementById('productsGrid');
    if (!products.length) {
        grid.innerHTML = '<div style="grid-column:1/-1;text-align:center;padding:3rem;color:var(--text-muted)">No se encontraron productos</div>';
        return;
    }
    document.getElementById('productCount').textContent = `${products.length} productos encontrados`;
    grid.innerHTML = products.map(p => {
        const stars = starsMarkup(p.rating);
        return `<div class="product-card" onclick="openModal(${p.id})">
            <div class="product-img">
                ${p.stock < 10 ? '<span class="product-badge">Pocas unidades</span>' : ''}
                ${p.img ? `<img src="${p.img}" style="width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover;position:absolute;top:0;left:0">` : `<span>${p.emoji}</span>`}
            </div>
            <div class="product-info">
                <div class="product-category">${p.category}</div>
                <div class="product-rating">${stars}</div>
                <div class="product-name">${p.name}</div>
                <div class="product-desc">${p.desc}</div>
                <div class="product-footer">
                    <div class="product-price">$${p.price.toLocaleString('es-CO')} <span>COP</span></div>
                    <button class="add-btn" onclick="event.stopPropagation();addToCart(${p.id})" title="Agregar"><i class="bi bi-plus-lg"></i></button>
                </div>
                ${p.stock < 5 ? `<div class="stock-low"><i class="bi bi-exclamation-triangle-fill"></i> Solo ${p.stock} disponibles</div>` : ''}
            </div>
        </div>`;
    }).join('');

    // Category filter
    const cats = [...new Set(PRODUCTS.filter(p=>p.active).map(p => p.category))];
    const sel = document.getElementById('categoryFilter');
    sel.innerHTML = '<option value="">Todas las categorías</option>';
    cats.forEach(c => sel.add(new Option(c, c)));
}

function filterProducts() {
    const q = document.getElementById('searchInput').value.toLowerCase();
    const cat = document.getElementById('categoryFilter').value;
    const filtered = PRODUCTS.filter(p => {
        const matchQ = !q || p.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || p.desc.toLowerCase().includes(q);
        const matchCat = !cat || p.category === cat;
        return p.active && matchQ && matchCat;
    });
    renderProducts(filtered);
}

// ══════════════════════════════════════════
// MODAL PRODUCTO
// ══════════════════════════════════════════
function openModal(id) {
    currentProduct = PRODUCTS.find(p => p.id === id);
    if (!currentProduct || !currentProduct.active) return;
    const p = currentProduct;
    const header = document.querySelector('#productModal .modal-header');
    // Reset header
    header.style.paddingTop = p.img ? '0' : '3rem';
    const existImg = header.querySelector('img');
    if (existImg) existImg.remove();
    if (p.img) {
        const img = document.createElement('img');
        img.src = p.img;
        img.style.cssText = 'width:100%;height:220px;object-fit:cover;border-radius:20px 20px 0 0;position:absolute;top:0;left:0';
        header.prepend(img);
    }
    document.getElementById('modalEmoji').textContent = p.img ? '' : p.emoji;
    document.getElementById('modalCategory').textContent = p.category;
    document.getElementById('modalName').textContent = p.name;
    document.getElementById('modalDesc').textContent = p.desc;
    document.getElementById('modalPrice').textContent = `$${p.price.toLocaleString('es-CO')} COP`;
    const stockEl = document.getElementById('modalStock');
    stockEl.innerHTML = p.stock > 10
        ? `<i class="bi bi-check-circle-fill"></i> ${p.stock} en stock`
        : p.stock > 0
            ? `<i class="bi bi-exclamation-triangle-fill"></i> Solo ${p.stock} disponibles`
            : '<i class="bi bi-x-circle-fill"></i> Sin stock';
    stockEl.className = 'modal-stock ' + (p.stock > 5 ? 'ok' : 'low');
    document.getElementById('qtyInput').value = 1;
    document.getElementById('reviewsList').innerHTML = p.reviews.length
        ? p.reviews.map(r => `<div class="review-item"><div class="review-header"><span class="review-author">${r.author}</span><span>${starsMarkup(r.stars)}</span></div><div class="review-text">${r.text}</div></div>`).join('')
        : '<div style="color:var(--text-muted);font-size:0.88rem">Sin reseñas aún.</div>';
    document.getElementById('productModal').classList.add('open');
}

function closeModal(e) {
    if (e.target === document.getElementById('productModal'))
        document.getElementById('productModal').classList.remove('open');
}
function changeQty(delta) {
    const input = document.getElementById('qtyInput');
    input.value = Math.max(1, parseInt(input.value) + delta);
}
function addToCartFromModal() {
    if (!currentProduct) return;
    addToCart(currentProduct.id, parseInt(document.getElementById('qtyInput').value) || 1);
    document.getElementById('productModal').classList.remove('open');
}

// ══════════════════════════════════════════
// CART
// ══════════════════════════════════════════
function addToCart(id, qty = 1) {
    const p = PRODUCTS.find(pr => pr.id === id);
    if (!p) return;
    const existing = cart.find(i => i.id === id);
    if (existing) existing.qty += qty; else cart.push({ id, qty });
    saveCart();
    updateCartCount();
    toast(`${p.name} agregado al carrito`, 'success');
}
function saveCart() { localStorage.setItem('cocina_cart', JSON.stringify(cart)); }
function updateCartCount() {
    const total = cart.reduce((s, i) => s + i.qty, 0);
    document.getElementById('cartCount').textContent = total;
}
function clearCart() { cart = []; saveCart(); updateCartCount(); renderCart(); toast('Carrito vaciado'); }
function renderCart() {
    const list = document.getElementById('cartItemsList');
    if (!cart.length) {
        list.innerHTML = `<div class="cart-empty"><div class="cart-empty-icon"><i class="bi bi-cart-x"></i></div><h3>Tu carrito está vacío</h3><p>Agrega productos del catálogo para empezar</p><button class="btn-primary" onclick="showSection('catalogo')">Ver Catálogo <i class="bi bi-arrow-right"></i></button></div>`;
        document.getElementById('summarySubtotal').textContent = '$0';
        document.getElementById('summaryTotal').textContent = '$0';
        return;
    }
    list.innerHTML = cart.map(item => {
        const p = PRODUCTS.find(pr => pr.id === item.id);
        return `<div class="cart-item">
            ${p.img ? `<img class="cart-item-img" src="${p.img}">` : `<div class="cart-item-emoji">${p.emoji}</div>`}
            <div class="cart-item-info"><div class="cart-item-name">${p.name}</div><div class="cart-item-price">$${p.price.toLocaleString('es-CO')} c/u</div></div>
            <div class="cart-item-qty">
                <button class="ciq-btn" onclick="updateQty(${p.id},-1)">−</button>
                <span class="ciq-num">${item.qty}</span>
                <button class="ciq-btn" onclick="updateQty(${p.id},1)">+</button>
            </div>
            <div class="cart-item-total">$${(p.price*item.qty).toLocaleString('es-CO')}</div>
            <button class="remove-btn" onclick="removeFromCart(${p.id})"><i class="bi bi-x-lg"></i></button>
        </div>`;
    }).join('');
    const subtotal = cart.reduce((s,i) => s + PRODUCTS.find(p=>p.id===i.id).price * i.qty, 0);
    document.getElementById('summarySubtotal').textContent = '$' + subtotal.toLocaleString('es-CO');
    document.getElementById('summaryTotal').textContent = '$' + subtotal.toLocaleString('es-CO');
}
function updateQty(id, delta) {
    const item = cart.find(i => i.id === id);
    if (!item) return;
    item.qty = Math.max(1, item.qty + delta);
    saveCart(); updateCartCount(); renderCart();
}
function removeFromCart(id) {
    cart = cart.filter(i => i.id !== id);
    saveCart(); updateCartCount(); renderCart(); toast('Producto eliminado');
}

// ══════════════════════════════════════════
// CHECKOUT / ORDERS
// ══════════════════════════════════════════
function procesarPedido(e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    const total = cart.reduce((s,i) => s + PRODUCTS.find(p=>p.id===i.id).price * i.qty, 0);
    const order = {
        id: 'ORD-' + Date.now(),
        date: new Date().toLocaleDateString('es-CO'),
        items: cart.map(i => ({...i, name: PRODUCTS.find(p=>p.id===i.id).name})),
        total,
        metodo: document.getElementById('metodoPago').value,
        direccion: document.getElementById('direccionEntrega').value,
        notas: document.getElementById('notasPedido').value,
        status: 'pending'
    };
    orders.unshift(order);
    localStorage.setItem('cocina_orders', JSON.stringify(orders));
    cart = []; saveCart(); updateCartCount();
    toast('Pedido confirmado. Gracias por tu compra.', 'success');
    showSection('misPedidos');
}
function renderOrders() {
    const list = document.getElementById('pedidosList');
    if (!orders.length) {
        list.innerHTML = `<div class="cart-empty"><div class="cart-empty-icon"><i class="bi bi-box-seam"></i></div><h3>No tienes pedidos aún</h3><p>Cuando realices tu primer pedido aparecerá aquí</p><button class="btn-primary" onclick="showSection('catalogo')">Ir al Catálogo <i class="bi bi-arrow-right"></i></button></div>`;
        return;
    }
    const statusMap = {pending:['status-pending','Pendiente'],shipped:['status-shipped','Enviado'],delivered:['status-delivered','Entregado'],cancelled:['status-cancelled','Cancelado']};
    list.innerHTML = orders.map(o => {
        const [cls,label] = statusMap[o.status] || statusMap.pending;
        return `<div class="pedido-card">
            <div class="pedido-header"><span class="pedido-id">${o.id}</span><span class="pedido-status ${cls}">${label}</span></div>
            <div class="pedido-items">${o.items.map(i=>`${i.name} ×${i.qty}`).join(', ')}</div>
            <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;margin-top:0.5rem">
                <span style="font-size:0.82rem;color:var(--text-light)">${o.date} · ${o.metodo}</span>
                <span class="pedido-total">$${o.total.toLocaleString('es-CO')} COP</span>
            </div>
        </div>`;
    }).join('');
}

// ══════════════════════════════════════════
// AUTH
// ══════════════════════════════════════════
async function registerUser(e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    updateRegisterSummary();
    if (!validateRegisterField('regNombre') || !validateRegisterField('regApellido') || !validateRegisterField('regEmail') || !validateRegisterField('regPass') || !validateRegisterField('regPassConfirm') || !validateRegisterField('regTel') || !validateRegisterField('regDir')) {
        updateRegisterStatus('Revisa los campos marcados antes de continuar.', 'error');
        toast('Completa correctamente todos los campos', 'error');
        return;
    }

    try {
        const result = await apiRequest('register', 'POST', {
            nombre: document.getElementById('regNombre').value.trim(),
            apellido: document.getElementById('regApellido').value.trim(),
            email: document.getElementById('regEmail').value.trim(),
            password: document.getElementById('regPass').value,
            confirmar_password: document.getElementById('regPassConfirm').value,
            telefono: document.getElementById('regTel').value.trim(),
            direccion: document.getElementById('regDir').value.trim(),
        });

        updateRegisterStatus(result.message || 'Usuario registrado en la base de datos.', 'success');
        e.target.reset();
        document.querySelectorAll('#registerForm .form-input, #registerForm .form-textarea').forEach(field => field.classList.remove('is-valid', 'is-invalid'));
        updateRegisterSummary();
        toast('Cuenta registrada en la base de datos', 'success');
        showSection('login');
    } catch (error) {
        updateRegisterStatus(error.message, 'error');
        toast(error.message, 'error');
    }
}

async function loginUser(e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    try {
        const result = await apiRequest('login', 'POST', {
            email: document.getElementById('loginEmail').value.trim(),
            password: document.getElementById('loginPass').value,
        });
        persistCurrentUser(result.data);
        toast(`Bienvenido ${formatUserLabel(result.data)} · ${result.data.rol || result.data.role}`, 'success');
        showSection('home');
    } catch (error) {
        toast(error.message, 'error');
    }
}
async function logoutUser() {
    try {
        await fetch('../api/auth.php?action=logout', {
            method: 'POST',
            credentials: 'same-origin',
            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }
        });
    } catch (_) {
        // Si el backend no responde, igual limpiamos el estado local.
    }

    const adminPanel = document.getElementById('adminPanel');
    if (adminPanel) adminPanel.classList.remove('active');
    persistCurrentUser(null);
    document.getElementById('publicNav').style.display = '';
    document.getElementById('publicFooter').style.display = '';
    showSection('login');
    toast('Sesión cerrada correctamente', 'success');
}
function guardarPerfil(e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!currentUser) { toast('Primero inicia sesión', 'error'); return; }
    const updatedUser = {
        ...currentUser,
        name: document.getElementById('perfilNombre').value.trim(),
        lastname: document.getElementById('perfilApellido').value.trim(),
        email: document.getElementById('perfilEmailInput').value.trim(),
        phone: document.getElementById('perfilTel').value.trim(),
        address: document.getElementById('perfilDir').value.trim(),
    };
    const idx = USERS.findIndex(user => user.id === currentUser.id);
    if (idx !== -1) {
        USERS[idx] = { ...USERS[idx], ...updatedUser };
        saveData();
    }
    persistCurrentUser(updatedUser);
    toast('Perfil actualizado correctamente','success');
}
function solicitarCodigo(e) { e.preventDefault(); toast('Código enviado a tu email','success'); }
function resetPassword(e) { e.preventDefault(); toast('Contraseña restablecida con éxito','success'); showSection('login'); }
function togglePass(inputId, btn) {
    const input = document.getElementById(inputId);
    if (input.type === 'password') { input.type='text'; btn.innerHTML='<i class="bi bi-eye-slash"></i>'; } else { input.type='password'; btn.innerHTML='<i class="bi bi-eye"></i>'; }
}

// ══════════════════════════════════════════
// ADMIN PANEL
// ══════════════════════════════════════════
function openAdmin() {
    if (!currentUser) {
        toast('Debes iniciar sesión para acceder al panel de administración', 'error');
        showSection('login');
        return;
    }
    if (!canAccessAdminPanel()) {
        toast('No tienes permisos para acceder al panel de administración', 'error');
        return;
    }
    document.querySelectorAll('.section').forEach(s => s.classList.remove('active'));
    document.getElementById('publicNav').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('publicFooter').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('adminPanel').classList.add('active');
    renderCurrentUser();
    showAdminPage('dashboard', document.querySelector('.admin-nav-item'));
}
function closeAdmin() { showSection('home'); }

function showAdminPage(pageId, navItem) {
    if (!canAccessAdminPanel()) {
        closeAdmin();
        toast('No tienes permisos para acceder al panel de administración', 'error');
        return;
    }
    document.querySelectorAll('.admin-page').forEach(p => p.classList.remove('active'));
    document.getElementById('page-' + pageId).classList.add('active');
    document.querySelectorAll('.admin-nav-item').forEach(i => i.classList.remove('active'));
    if (navItem) navItem.classList.add('active');
    const titles = {dashboard:'Dashboard',products:'Productos',categories:'Categorías',offers:'Ofertas',orders:'Pedidos',users:'Usuarios'};
    document.getElementById('adminPageTitle').textContent = titles[pageId] || pageId;
    // Render
    if (pageId === 'dashboard') renderDashboard();
    if (pageId === 'products') renderProductsTable();
    if (pageId === 'categories') renderCategoriesTable();
    if (pageId === 'offers') renderOffersTable();
    if (pageId === 'orders') renderOrdersTable();
    if (pageId === 'users') renderUsersTable();
}

// ─── DASHBOARD ───
function renderDashboard() {
    const totalRevenue = orders.reduce((s,o) => s + o.total, 0);
    const pendingOrders = orders.filter(o => o.status === 'pending').length;
    const lowStock = PRODUCTS.filter(p => p.stock < 5).length;
    document.getElementById('dashStats').innerHTML = `
        <div class="stat-card"><div class="stat-card-label">Ingresos Totales</div><div class="stat-card-value">$${(totalRevenue/1000).toFixed(0)}k</div><div class="stat-card-change change-up">↑ COP</div><div class="stat-card-icon"><i class="bi bi-cash-stack"></i></div></div>
        <div class="stat-card"><div class="stat-card-label">Pedidos Totales</div><div class="stat-card-value">${orders.length}</div><div class="stat-card-change">${pendingOrders} pendientes</div><div class="stat-card-icon"><i class="bi bi-box-seam"></i></div></div>
        <div class="stat-card"><div class="stat-card-label">Productos</div><div class="stat-card-value">${PRODUCTS.filter(p=>p.active).length}</div><div class="stat-card-change">${lowStock} con stock bajo</div><div class="stat-card-icon"><i class="bi bi-bag-check"></i></div></div>
        <div class="stat-card"><div class="stat-card-label">Usuarios</div><div class="stat-card-value">${USERS.length}</div><div class="stat-card-change change-up">${USERS.filter(u=>u.active).length} activos</div><div class="stat-card-icon"><i class="bi bi-people"></i></div></div>
    `;
    const statusMap = {pending:['status-pending','Pendiente'],shipped:['status-shipped','Enviado'],delivered:['status-delivered','Entregado'],cancelled:['status-cancelled','Cancelado']};
    const recentOrders = orders.slice(0,5);
    document.getElementById('dashRecentOrders').innerHTML = recentOrders.length
        ? `<table class="data-table"><thead><tr><th>ID</th><th>Artículos</th><th>Total</th><th>Estado</th></tr></thead><tbody>${recentOrders.map(o=>{const[cls,label]=statusMap[o.status]||statusMap.pending;return`<tr><td style="font-weight:600;font-size:0.82rem">${o.id}</td><td style="font-size:0.82rem;color:var(--text-muted)">${o.items.length} ítem(s)</td><td style="font-weight:600">$${o.total.toLocaleString('es-CO')}</td><td><span class="pedido-status ${cls}">${label}</span></td></tr>`}).join('')}</tbody></table>`
        : '<div style="padding:2rem;text-align:center;color:var(--text-muted);font-size:0.88rem">No hay pedidos aún</div>';
    const lowStockProducts = PRODUCTS.filter(p => p.stock < 8).sort((a,b)=>a.stock-b.stock).slice(0,6);
    document.getElementById('dashLowStock').innerHTML = lowStockProducts.length
        ? `<table class="data-table"><thead><tr><th>Producto</th><th>Stock</th></tr></thead><tbody>${lowStockProducts.map(p=>`<tr><td><div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px"><span>${p.emoji}</span><span style="font-size:0.85rem;font-weight:500">${p.name}</span></div></td><td><span class="stock-badge ${p.stock===0?'stock-out':p.stock<5?'stock-low':'stock-ok'}">${p.stock} uds</span></td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table>`
        : '<div style="padding:2rem;text-align:center;color:var(--text-muted);font-size:0.88rem"><i class="bi bi-check2-circle"></i> Stock OK en todos los productos</div>';
}

// ─── PRODUCTS TABLE ───
function renderProductsTable() {
    const q = (document.getElementById('adminSearchProduct')?.value || '').toLowerCase();
    const cat = document.getElementById('adminCatFilter')?.value || '';
    const filtered = PRODUCTS.filter(p => {
        const matchQ = !q || p.name.toLowerCase().includes(q);
        const matchCat = !cat || p.category === cat;
        return matchQ && matchCat;
    });
    document.getElementById('productsTableBody').innerHTML = filtered.map(p => `
        <tr>
            <td><div class="product-thumb-wrap">
                <div class="product-thumb">${p.img?`<img src="${p.img}">`:`<span>${p.emoji}</span>`}</div>
                <div><div class="product-thumb-name">${p.name}</div><div class="product-thumb-cat">#${p.id}</div></div>
            </div></td>
            <td><span class="badge badge-gray">${p.category}</span></td>
            <td style="font-weight:600">$${p.price.toLocaleString('es-CO')}</td>
            <td><span class="stock-badge ${p.stock===0?'stock-out':p.stock<5?'stock-low':'stock-ok'}">${p.stock} uds</span></td>
            <td>${starsMarkup(p.rating)}</td>
            <td><span class="badge ${p.active?'badge-green':'badge-gray'}">${p.active?'Activo':'Inactivo'}</span></td>
            <td>
                <div class="action-btns">
                    <button class="btn-icon" title="Editar" onclick="openProductModal(${p.id})"><i class="bi bi-pencil-square"></i></button>
                    <button class="btn-icon" title="${p.active?'Desactivar':'Activar'}" onclick="toggleProductActive(${p.id})">${p.active?'<i class="bi bi-lock-fill"></i>':'<i class="bi bi-unlock-fill"></i>'}</button>
                    <button class="btn-icon danger" title="Eliminar" onclick="confirmDelete('product',${p.id})"><i class="bi bi-trash3"></i></button>
                </div>
            </td>
        </tr>`).join('');
    // Populate category filter
    const sel = document.getElementById('adminCatFilter');
    const current = sel.value;
    sel.innerHTML = '<option value="">Todas las categorías</option>';
    CATEGORIES.forEach(c => sel.add(new Option(c.name, c.name)));
    sel.value = current;
}

// ─── PRODUCT MODAL ───
function openProductModal(id = null) {
    editingId = id;
    uploadedImageData = null;
    document.getElementById('productModalTitle').textContent = id ? 'Editar Producto' : 'Nuevo Producto';
    // Populate categories
    const sel = document.getElementById('pCategory');
    sel.innerHTML = '<option value="">Seleccionar...</option>';
    CATEGORIES.forEach(c => sel.add(new Option(c.name, c.name)));
    // Reset image area
    document.getElementById('imgUploadContent').innerHTML = `
        <div class="img-upload-icon"><i class="bi bi-camera"></i></div>
        <div class="img-upload-text">Haz clic para subir una imagen</div>
        <div class="img-upload-sub">JPG, PNG, WEBP — máx. 5MB</div>`;
    document.getElementById('imgUploadArea').classList.remove('has-img');
    document.getElementById('pActiveToggle').classList.add('on');
    if (id) {
        const p = PRODUCTS.find(pr => pr.id === id);
        document.getElementById('pName').value = p.name;
        document.getElementById('pEmoji').value = p.emoji;
        document.getElementById('pCategory').value = p.category;
        document.getElementById('pPrice').value = p.price;
        document.getElementById('pStock').value = p.stock;
        document.getElementById('pRating').value = p.rating;
        document.getElementById('pDesc').value = p.desc;
        if (!p.active) document.getElementById('pActiveToggle').classList.remove('on');
        if (p.img) {
            uploadedImageData = p.img;
            document.getElementById('imgUploadContent').innerHTML = `<img src="${p.img}" class="img-preview"><div class="img-upload-text">Imagen cargada <i class="bi bi-check-circle-fill"></i></div><div class="img-upload-sub">Haz clic para cambiar</div>`;
            document.getElementById('imgUploadArea').classList.add('has-img');
        }
    } else {
        ['pName','pEmoji','pPrice','pStock','pDesc'].forEach(f => document.getElementById(f).value = '');
        document.getElementById('pRating').value = '5';
        document.getElementById('pCategory').value = '';
    }
    openAdminModal('modalProduct');
}

function handleImgUpload(event) {
    const file = event.target.files[0];
    if (!file) return;
    if (file.size > 5 * 1024 * 1024) { toast('La imagen supera el límite de 5MB', 'error'); return; }
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = (e) => {
        uploadedImageData = e.target.result;
        document.getElementById('imgUploadContent').innerHTML = `<img src="${uploadedImageData}" class="img-preview"><div class="img-upload-text">Imagen cargada <i class="bi bi-check-circle-fill"></i></div><div class="img-upload-sub">Haz clic para cambiar</div>`;
        document.getElementById('imgUploadArea').classList.add('has-img');
    };
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
}

function saveProduct() {
    const name = document.getElementById('pName').value.trim();
    const category = document.getElementById('pCategory').value;
    const price = parseInt(document.getElementById('pPrice').value);
    const stock = parseInt(document.getElementById('pStock').value);
    const desc = document.getElementById('pDesc').value.trim();
    if (!name || !category || !price || isNaN(stock) || !desc) { toast('Completa todos los campos obligatorios', 'error'); return; }
    const emoji = document.getElementById('pEmoji').value || '📦';
    const rating = parseInt(document.getElementById('pRating').value);
    const active = document.getElementById('pActiveToggle').classList.contains('on');
    if (editingId) {
        const idx = PRODUCTS.findIndex(p => p.id === editingId);
        PRODUCTS[idx] = { ...PRODUCTS[idx], name, category, price, stock, desc, emoji, rating, active, img: uploadedImageData || PRODUCTS[idx].img };
        toast('Producto actualizado correctamente', 'success');
    } else {
        const newId = Math.max(...PRODUCTS.map(p=>p.id), 0) + 1;
        PRODUCTS.push({ id:newId, name, category, price, stock, desc, emoji, rating, active, img:uploadedImageData||null, reviews:[] });
        toast('Producto creado correctamente', 'success');
    }
    saveData(); closeAdminModal('modalProduct'); renderProductsTable();
}

function toggleProductActive(id) {
    const p = PRODUCTS.find(pr => pr.id === id);
    if (!p) return;
    p.active = !p.active;
    saveData(); renderProductsTable();
    toast(`Producto ${p.active?'activado':'desactivado'}`, p.active?'success':'');
}

// ─── CATEGORIES ───
function renderCategoriesTable() {
    document.getElementById('categoriesTableBody').innerHTML = CATEGORIES.map(c => {
        const count = PRODUCTS.filter(p => p.category === c.name).length;
        return `<tr>
            <td><div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px"><span style="font-size:1.3rem">${c.emoji}</span><span style="font-weight:500">${c.name}</span></div></td>
            <td><span class="badge badge-blue">${count} producto(s)</span></td>
            <td><div class="action-btns">
                <button class="btn-icon" onclick="openCategoryModal(${c.id})"><i class="bi bi-pencil-square"></i></button>
                <button class="btn-icon danger" onclick="confirmDelete('category',${c.id})"><i class="bi bi-trash3"></i></button>
            </div></td>
        </tr>`;
    }).join('');
}

function openCategoryModal(id = null) {
    editingId = id;
    document.getElementById('categoryModalTitle').textContent = id ? 'Editar Categoría' : 'Nueva Categoría';
    if (id) {
        const c = CATEGORIES.find(cat => cat.id === id);
        document.getElementById('cName').value = c.name;
        document.getElementById('cEmoji').value = c.emoji;
    } else {
        document.getElementById('cName').value = '';
        document.getElementById('cEmoji').value = '';
    }
    openAdminModal('modalCategory');
}

function saveCategory() {
    const name = document.getElementById('cName').value.trim();
    const emoji = document.getElementById('cEmoji').value || '🏷️';
    if (!name) { toast('Ingresa el nombre de la categoría', 'error'); return; }
    if (editingId) {
        const c = CATEGORIES.find(cat => cat.id === editingId);
        const oldName = c.name;
        c.name = name; c.emoji = emoji;
        PRODUCTS.forEach(p => { if (p.category === oldName) p.category = name; });
        toast('Categoría actualizada correctamente', 'success');
    } else {
        CATEGORIES.push({ id: Math.max(...CATEGORIES.map(c=>c.id), 0)+1, name, emoji });
        toast('Categoría creada correctamente', 'success');
    }
    saveData(); closeAdminModal('modalCategory'); renderCategoriesTable();
}

// ─── OFFERS ───
function renderOffersTable() {
    document.getElementById('offersTableBody').innerHTML = OFFERS.map((o,idx) => {
        const p = PRODUCTS.find(pr => pr.id === o.id);
        if (!p) return '';
        const final = Math.round(p.price * (1 - o.discount/100));
        return `<tr>
            <td><div class="product-thumb-wrap">
                <div class="product-thumb">${p.img?`<img src="${p.img}">`:`<span>${p.emoji}</span>`}</div>
                <div class="product-thumb-name">${p.name}</div>
            </div></td>
            <td>$${p.price.toLocaleString('es-CO')}</td>
            <td><span class="badge badge-amber">-${o.discount}%</span></td>
            <td style="color:var(--amber);font-weight:700">$${final.toLocaleString('es-CO')}</td>
            <td><div class="action-btns">
                <button class="btn-icon danger" onclick="confirmDelete('offer',${idx})"><i class="bi bi-trash3"></i></button>
            </div></td>
        </tr>`;
    }).join('');
}

function openOfferModal() {
    const sel = document.getElementById('ofProduct');
    sel.innerHTML = '<option value="">Seleccionar producto...</option>';
    PRODUCTS.filter(p=>p.active).forEach(p => sel.add(new Option(p.emoji + ' ' + p.name, p.id)));
    document.getElementById('ofDiscount').value = '';
    document.getElementById('offerPreview').style.display = 'none';
    openAdminModal('modalOffer');
}

function previewOffer() {
    const id = parseInt(document.getElementById('ofProduct').value);
    const disc = parseInt(document.getElementById('ofDiscount').value);
    if (!id || !disc || disc < 1 || disc > 99) { document.getElementById('offerPreview').style.display = 'none'; return; }
    const p = PRODUCTS.find(pr => pr.id === id);
    document.getElementById('offerPreview').style.display = 'block';
    document.getElementById('ofPreviewEmoji').textContent = p.emoji;
    document.getElementById('ofPreviewName').textContent = p.name;
    document.getElementById('ofPreviewOld').textContent = '$' + p.price.toLocaleString('es-CO');
    document.getElementById('ofPreviewNew').textContent = '$' + Math.round(p.price*(1-disc/100)).toLocaleString('es-CO') + ' COP';
}

function saveOffer() {
    const id = parseInt(document.getElementById('ofProduct').value);
    const discount = parseInt(document.getElementById('ofDiscount').value);
    if (!id || !discount || discount < 1 || discount > 99) { toast('Completa los campos correctamente', 'error'); return; }
    if (OFFERS.find(o => o.id === id)) { toast('Este producto ya tiene una oferta activa', 'error'); return; }
    OFFERS.push({ id, discount });
    saveData(); closeAdminModal('modalOffer'); renderOffersTable();
    toast('Oferta creada correctamente', 'success');
}

// ─── ORDERS (admin) ───
function renderOrdersTable() {
    const q = (document.getElementById('adminSearchOrder')?.value || '').toLowerCase();
    const status = document.getElementById('adminStatusFilter')?.value || '';
    const filtered = orders.filter(o => {
        const matchQ = !q || o.id.toLowerCase().includes(q);
        const matchS = !status || o.status === status;
        return matchQ && matchS;
    });
    const statusMap = {pending:['status-pending','Pendiente'],shipped:['status-shipped','Enviado'],delivered:['status-delivered','Entregado'],cancelled:['status-cancelled','Cancelado']};
    document.getElementById('ordersTableBody').innerHTML = filtered.length
        ? filtered.map(o => {
            const [cls,label] = statusMap[o.status] || statusMap.pending;
            return `<tr>
                <td style="font-weight:700;font-size:0.82rem">${o.id}</td>
                <td>${o.date}</td>
                <td>${o.metodo}</td>
                <td>${o.items.map(i=>`${i.name} ×${i.qty}`).join('<br>')}</td>
                <td style="font-weight:700">$${o.total.toLocaleString('es-CO')}</td>
                <td><span class="pedido-status ${cls}">${label}</span></td>
                <td>
                    <select class="table-filter" style="font-size:0.78rem;padding:0.3rem 0.5rem" onchange="updateOrderStatus('${o.id}',this.value)">
                        <option value="pending" ${o.status==='pending'?'selected':''}>Pendiente</option>
                        <option value="shipped" ${o.status==='shipped'?'selected':''}>Enviado</option>
                        <option value="delivered" ${o.status==='delivered'?'selected':''}>Entregado</option>
                        <option value="cancelled" ${o.status==='cancelled'?'selected':''}>Cancelado</option>
                    </select>
                </td>
            </tr>`;}).join('')
        : '<tr><td colspan="7" style="text-align:center;color:var(--text-muted);padding:2rem">No hay pedidos</td></tr>';
}

function updateOrderStatus(orderId, status) {
    const order = orders.find(o => o.id === orderId);
    if (order) { order.status = status; localStorage.setItem('cocina_orders', JSON.stringify(orders)); toast('Estado actualizado correctamente', 'success'); renderOrdersTable(); }
}

// ─── USERS ───
function renderUsersTable() {
    const q = (document.getElementById('adminSearchUser')?.value || '').toLowerCase();
    const role = document.getElementById('adminRoleFilter')?.value || '';
    const filtered = USERS.filter(u => {
        const matchQ = !q || `${u.name} ${u.lastname} ${u.email}`.toLowerCase().includes(q);
        const matchR = !role || u.role === role;
        return matchQ && matchR;
    });
    document.getElementById('usersTableBody').innerHTML = filtered.map(u => `
        <tr>
            <td><div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px">
                <div class="user-avatar-sm">${u.name[0]}${u.lastname[0]}</div>
                <div><div style="font-weight:600;font-size:0.88rem">${u.name} ${u.lastname}</div><div style="font-size:0.75rem;color:var(--text-light)">${u.phone||'—'}</div></div>
            </div></td>
            <td style="font-size:0.85rem">${u.email}</td>
            <td><span class="badge ${u.role==='admin'?'badge-amber':'badge-blue'}">${u.role==='admin'?'Admin':'Cliente'}</span></td>
            <td>${u.orders||0}</td>
            <td><span class="badge ${u.active?'badge-green':'badge-red'}">${u.active?'Activo':'Bloqueado'}</span></td>
            <td><div class="action-btns">
                <button class="btn-icon" title="Editar" onclick="openUserModal(${u.id})"><i class="bi bi-pencil-square"></i></button>
                <button class="btn-icon ${u.active?'danger':'success'}" title="${u.active?'Bloquear':'Desbloquear'}" onclick="toggleUserBlock(${u.id})">${u.active?'<i class="bi bi-lock-fill"></i>':'<i class="bi bi-unlock-fill"></i>'}</button>
                <button class="btn-icon danger" title="Eliminar" onclick="confirmDelete('user',${u.id})"><i class="bi bi-trash3"></i></button>
            </div></td>
        </tr>`).join('');
}

function openUserModal(id = null) {
    editingId = id;
    document.getElementById('userModalTitle').textContent = id ? 'Editar Usuario' : 'Nuevo Usuario';
    if (id) {
        const u = USERS.find(us => us.id === id);
        document.getElementById('uName').value = u.name;
        document.getElementById('uLastname').value = u.lastname;
        document.getElementById('uEmail').value = u.email;
        document.getElementById('uRole').value = u.role;
        document.getElementById('uPhone').value = u.phone || '';
    } else {
        ['uName','uLastname','uEmail','uPhone'].forEach(f => document.getElementById(f).value = '');
        document.getElementById('uRole').value = 'cliente';
    }
    openAdminModal('modalUser');
}

function saveUser() {
    const name = document.getElementById('uName').value.trim();
    const lastname = document.getElementById('uLastname').value.trim();
    const email = document.getElementById('uEmail').value.trim();
    const role = document.getElementById('uRole').value;
    const phone = document.getElementById('uPhone').value.trim();
    if (!name || !lastname || !email) { toast('Completa los campos obligatorios', 'error'); return; }
    if (editingId) {
        const u = USERS.find(us => us.id === editingId);
        Object.assign(u, { name, lastname, email, role, phone });
        toast('Usuario actualizado correctamente', 'success');
    } else {
        USERS.push({ id: Math.max(...USERS.map(u=>u.id),0)+1, name, lastname, email, role, phone, active:true, orders:0 });
        toast('Usuario creado correctamente', 'success');
    }
    saveData(); closeAdminModal('modalUser'); renderUsersTable();
}

function toggleUserBlock(id) {
    const u = USERS.find(us => us.id === id);
    if (!u) return;
    u.active = !u.active;
    saveData(); renderUsersTable();
    toast(`Usuario ${u.active?'desbloqueado':'bloqueado'}`, u.active?'success':'error');
}

// ─── CONFIRM DELETE ───
function confirmDelete(type, id) {
    const configs = {
        product: { icon:'bi-trash3-fill', title:'¿Eliminar producto?', msg:'El producto será eliminado permanentemente de la tienda.', btn:'Eliminar Producto' },
        category: { icon:'bi-tags-fill', title:'¿Eliminar categoría?', msg:'Esto no eliminará los productos de esta categoría.', btn:'Eliminar Categoría' },
        offer: { icon:'bi-percent', title:'¿Eliminar oferta?', msg:'La oferta será desactivada inmediatamente.', btn:'Eliminar Oferta' },
        user: { icon:'bi-person-x-fill', title:'¿Eliminar usuario?', msg:'El usuario y todos sus datos serán eliminados.', btn:'Eliminar Usuario' },
    };
    const cfg = configs[type];
    document.getElementById('confirmIcon').innerHTML = `<i class="bi ${cfg.icon}"></i>`;
    document.getElementById('confirmTitle').textContent = cfg.title;
    document.getElementById('confirmMsg').textContent = cfg.msg;
    document.getElementById('confirmBtn').textContent = cfg.btn;
    document.getElementById('confirmBtn').onclick = () => { deleteItem(type, id); closeAdminModal('modalConfirm'); };
    openAdminModal('modalConfirm');
}

function deleteItem(type, id) {
    if (type === 'product') { PRODUCTS = PRODUCTS.filter(p => p.id !== id); renderProductsTable(); }
    if (type === 'category') { CATEGORIES = CATEGORIES.filter(c => c.id !== id); renderCategoriesTable(); }
    if (type === 'offer') { OFFERS.splice(id, 1); renderOffersTable(); }
    if (type === 'user') { USERS = USERS.filter(u => u.id !== id); renderUsersTable(); }
    saveData(); toast('Eliminado correctamente', 'success');
}

// ─── MODAL HELPERS ───
function openAdminModal(id) { document.getElementById(id).classList.add('open'); }
function closeAdminModal(id) { document.getElementById(id).classList.remove('open'); }
document.querySelectorAll('.admin-modal-overlay').forEach(overlay => {
    overlay.addEventListener('click', e => { if (e.target === overlay) overlay.classList.remove('open'); });
});

// ══════════════════════════════════════════
// INIT
// ══════════════════════════════════════════
updateCartCount();
renderHeroVisual();
renderOfertas();
renderProducts();
updateHeroStats();
renderCurrentUser();
if (currentUser) syncUserInputsFromCurrentUser();
document.querySelectorAll('#registerForm .form-input, #registerForm .form-textarea').forEach(field => {
    field.addEventListener('input', () => {
        validateRegisterField(field.id);
        if (field.id === 'regEmail') {
            clearTimeout(emailAvailabilityTimer);
            emailAvailabilityTimer = setTimeout(() => validateRegisterEmailAvailability(), 350);
        }
    });
    field.addEventListener('blur', () => {
        validateRegisterField(field.id);
        if (field.id === 'regEmail') {
            validateRegisterEmailAvailability();
        }
    });
});
updateRegisterSummary();
</script>

          Evidence 
          <!-- /admin-content -->
          Solution

          Eliminar todos los comentarios que muestren información que pueda ayudar a un atacante y solucionar el problema al que se refieren.

Appendix

Alert Types

This section contains additional information on the types of alerts in the report.

  1. CSP: script-src unsafe-inline

    Source raised by a passive scanner (CSP)
    CWE ID 693
    WASC ID 15
    Reference
    1. https://www.w3.org/TR/CSP/
    2. https://caniuse.com/#search=content+security+policy
    3. https://content-security-policy.com/
    4. https://github.com/HtmlUnit/htmlunit-csp
    5. https://web.dev/articles/csp#resource-options

  2. CSP: style-src unsafe-inline

    Source raised by a passive scanner (CSP)
    CWE ID 693
    WASC ID 15
    Reference
    1. https://www.w3.org/TR/CSP/
    2. https://caniuse.com/#search=content+security+policy
    3. https://content-security-policy.com/
    4. https://github.com/HtmlUnit/htmlunit-csp
    5. https://web.dev/articles/csp#resource-options

  3. Cabecera Content Security Policy (CSP) no configurada

    Source raised by a passive scanner (Cabecera Content Security Policy (CSP) no configurada)
    CWE ID 693
    WASC ID 15
    Reference
    1. https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTTP/Guides/CSP
    2. https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/Content_Security_Policy_Cheat_Sheet.html
    3. https://www.w3.org/TR/CSP/
    4. https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-csp/
    5. https://web.dev/articles/csp
    6. https://caniuse.com/#feat=contentsecuritypolicy
    7. https://content-security-policy.com/

  4. El servidor filtra información de versión a través del campo "Server" del encabezado de respuesta HTTP

    Source raised by a passive scanner (Cabecera de Respuesta del Servidor HTTP)
    CWE ID 497
    WASC ID 13
    Reference
    1. https://httpd.apache.org/docs/current/mod/core.html#servertokens
    2. https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/previous-versions/msp-n-p/ff648552(v=pandp.10)
    3. https://www.troyhunt.com/shhh-dont-let-your-response-headers/

  5. Falta encabezado X-Content-Type-Options

    Source raised by a passive scanner (Falta encabezado X-Content-Type-Options)
    CWE ID 693
    WASC ID 15
    Reference
    1. https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/previous-versions/windows/internet-explorer/ie-developer/compatibility/gg622941(v=vs.85)
    2. https://owasp.org/www-community/Security_Headers

  6. Fuga de información en el Banner de la Página

    Source raised by a passive scanner (Fuga de información en el Banner de la Página)
    CWE ID 497
    WASC ID 13
    Reference
    1. https://owasp.org/www-project-web-security-testing-guide/v41/4-Web_Application_Security_Testing/08-Testing_for_Error_Handling/

  7. Aplicación Web Moderna

    Source raised by a passive scanner (Aplicación Web Moderna)

  8. Divulgación de información - Comentarios sospechosos

    Source raised by a passive scanner (Divulgación de información - Comentarios sospechosos)
    CWE ID 615
    WASC ID 13
